# Notebook colour contrast -- measured

Generated by `app/src/pages/journal-2-0/a11y/notebookContrast.test.js` at `83568a12d2` (wave 8, lane 8A, A5).
Do not edit by hand; regenerate:

```sh
cd app && NOTEBOOK_CONTRAST_DOC=1 npx vitest run src/pages/journal-2-0/a11y/notebookContrast.test.js
```

**Method.** Every rule in the 101 derived Notebook stylesheets (`a11y/cssAudit.js deriveNotebookCss`),
comments stripped. TEXT: the rule's `color` against its own background, else against `--bg`, `--bg-surface`
and `--bg-elevated` (worst of the three); translucent colours composited onto the surface first. Bars:
4.5:1 normal text, 3:1 large text (>= 24px, or >= 18.66px at weight 700+, read from the same rule),
3:1 non-text UI (focus indicators, input borders, the graph selection ring). Tokens are read from
`app/src/styles/tokens.css` `:root` (dark), `[data-theme="oled"]` and `[data-theme="light"]`, `var()` chains resolved.
The ONE formula is `app/src/styles/__tests__/contrastMath.js`.

**Counts.** 1147 measured pairs x 3 themes; 12 keyword colours (inherit, currentColor, transparent) counted, not measured.

## Expected failures (need a tokens.css ruling)


<details><summary>Every expected failure</summary>

| pair | theme | measured | ruling |
|---|---|---|---|

</details>

## Another lane's to fix

None. Every pair in another lane's stylesheet meets its bar, or is one of the token-level rows above.

## Measured over a picture, not the page

These are composited over black AND white (the extremes of the image underneath):

- `components/notebook/HeroImagePicker.module.css .iconBtn` -- Replace / Remove buttons drawn over the note's hero IMAGE
- `components/notebook/HeroImagePicker.module.css .dropHint` -- the "drop to replace" hint drawn over the hero IMAGE while a file is dragged
- `components/notebook/WidgetEmbedView.module.css .asOfChip` -- the as-of date drawn over a chart SNAPSHOT (its time-axis corner)
